70 /**
71 @ingroup kumi_functional
72 @brief Binds a certain amount of values into a callable reducing it's arity.
73
74 @param c Callable object to be bound.
75 @param ts elements to be used as prefilled arguments to c
76 @return A new callable matching C with ts arguments prefilled by the front.
77
78 @note Replaces std::bind_front to avoid depending on `functional`.
79 **/
80 //====================================================================================================================
81 template<typename C, typename... Ts>
82 KUMI_ABI constexpr auto bind_front(C&& c, Ts&&... ts)
83 noexcept(std::is_nothrow_move_constructible_v<std::decay_t<C>> &&
84 (std::is_nothrow_move_constructible_v<std::decay_t<Ts>> && ...))
85 requires(std::is_move_constructible_v<std::decay_t<C>> && (std::is_move_constructible_v<std::decay_t<Ts>> && ...))
86 {
87 168 return kumi::_::bind_t<kumi::_::Binding::front, std::decay_t<C>, std::decay_t<Ts>...>{KUMI_FWD(c), KUMI_FWD(ts)...};
88 }
